Root cause: `GENRECOMP_CALLTRACE=lo:hi`, frames without an entry of `ProcessFloorType $00603C` (once per GameLoop pass) and of `WaitUntilVBlank $000B5E` = overload frames (the pass ran past the VBlank). `HandleVBlankInterrupt $0011DE` always clears the wait bit; the game turns the display off during its fast uploads while the main loop still runs, so LAGSTAT had filed these as 'display off' (~19 % of room 5 frames). Passes in frames 720..999: 241 / 280 normal, 254 / 280 with `LANDSTALKER_VDPWAIT=0`; frames 815..858: 34 / 44 (WIDE 19 TALL 1), 37 / 44 (WIDE 0).

## Native Pace: the room 5 / room 23 slow motion fix (2026-10-30 morning)
Tool: `tools/patch_nativepace.py` (marker `LK_NATIVEPACE v1` in 60e24dc, `v2` in 617875f = menu/file hook). Env: `LANDSTALKER_NATIVEPACE=1|0` (wins over the file), `LANDSTALKER_NATIVEPACE_CAP=<lines>` (default 262), `LANDSTALKER_NATIVEPACE_STAT=1` (stdout once per second: `nativepace: 60 frames, held H, extra lines L, cap hit C`). Menu: Options > Quality of Life > 'Native Pace' (option id 40 in `platform_option_get`, `~/.landstalker_nativepace` '0' / '1', read live; de.json 'Nativ-Tempo'); `verify_build.sh` 71/71 incl. the marker and label. Own timing mode only.
Logic: in `bus_advance_inner`'s line loop, if the next line is the VBlank line and bit 7 of `$FF0F8B` is clear (main loop not yet in `WaitUntilVBlank $000B5E`) the clock is held at the end of the last visible line, the extra 68K cycles are not counted; after CAP lines in one frame VBlank comes anyway.
Headless test (harness `/tmp/np`): copy `~/aufnahmen/save_20261030_0318.srm`, input = first 300 frames of `rec_20261030_0318.txt` + idle, `LANDSTALKER_QSAVE=1 LANDSTALKER_QSREQ=l600`, hold left `800 0004`, `--ram-dump DIR:1`, Nigel x = `$FF5400 * 16 + Player_SubX $FF5402` (bytes swapped in the dump). Baseline dx `2 2 2 0 2 0 2 2 ...`; with Native Pace 2 every frame (a 0 is compensated by a 4). Room 5: 7..21 held frames per second, ~10-20 extra lines each, no cap hits in the room (only around the quick load).
Long run: `rec_20261029_1729` (43 201 frames) with Native Pace: no crash / hang, 2 min 52 s headless; 32 of 720 seconds had cap hits (open: probably loads / fades / waits without the wait bit); replay desyncs on purpose.
Deck: launcher `~/Landstalker_NativTempo.sh` (= `~/Landstalker.sh` + `LANDSTALKER_NATIVEPACE=1`): user 'passt' in room 5 and room 23 (SWAMP_SHRINE_23). Default stays Off in v1.3.0, later On after more testing.
Side fix: `~/Landstalker_Aufnahme.sh` writes the save copy back into `landstalker.srm` at game end (mode 'normal'; backup `.vor_<stamp>` first; script backup `.bak_20261030`); user's save got the room 5 quick save (backup `landstalker.srm.vor_20261030_raum5`).

## CRT Pro cost and the worker pool, 2026-10-29 (Sonnet 5.5; HANDOFF 0a165)
Cause of the micro stutter candidate (HANDOFF 0a164): the CPU display filters (CRT Pro / Curved most of all) cost 9 ms in the present step and every pass created and joined its SDL threads again each frame. Measured windowed in real time with the user's settings (display file `0 3 19 1`, WIDE 16, TALL 1, recording `rec_20261023_1739`, 2900 frames, isolated HOME, average of the per second `framestat:` lines minus the first two; jitter = mean of the per second sd; slow = frames over 20 ms in 46 s):

| filter | present before -> after (ms) | frames/s before -> after | jitter sd before -> after (ms) | slow frames before -> after |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 Off | 3.15 -> 2.78 | 60.00 -> 60.00 | 0.010 -> 0.010 | 0 -> 0 |
| 1 Scanlines | 3.55 -> 3.07 | 60.00 -> 60.00 | 0.011 -> 0.010 | 0 -> 0 |
| 2 CRT | 3.53 -> 3.06 | 60.00 -> 60.00 | 0.010 -> 0.010 | 0 -> 0 |
| 3 CRT Pro | 8.95 -> 4.29 | 59.91 -> 60.00 | 0.368 -> 0.010 | 2 -> 0 |
| 4 CRT Curved | 9.04 -> 5.75 | 59.85 -> 60.00 | 0.419 -> 0.011 | 3 -> 0 |

Single thread (`LANDSTALKER_FXTHREADS=1`) filter 3: present 19.6 ms before, 11.0 ms after; i.e. the hot loop got about 2x cheaper and the threads now scale (4 threads 5.5 ms, 8 threads 4.3 ms).
What changed (all output bit identical, see below):
- `tools/patch_display.py` marker `LK_FXPOOL v1`: persistent worker pool `lk_pool_run` (SDL threads + `SDL_sem` + `SDL_atomic`, created on first use, woken once per pass; a busy flag makes a second caller run the jobs itself). Used by the CRT filters (`lk_fx_render_mt`), de-dither (`lk_dd_par_run`) and, in `tools/patch_hd.py`, the depth blur (`lk_dd2_par`) and look (`lk_look_par`). The CRT passes hand out row chunks from a shared atomic counter (a late thread does fewer chunks), default thread count = CPU count up to 8 (was 4; `LANDSTALKER_FXTHREADS` as before).
- `tools/display_core.c` (marker comment `LK_FXFAST v1`): CRT Pro / Curved horizontal pass decodes gamma (and averages the HD rows) once per SOURCE pixel instead of per tap, tap columns and fractions come from a table made in `lk_f2_prepare` (no 64 bit division per pixel), the beam brightness class is stored in the 4th slot of the plane (it was computed twice per output pixel); vertical pass has a fast path for rows where gain is 255, column = x and the source row position is constant (CRT Pro; checked per row in `lk_f2_prepare`, Curved keeps the general path with the class from the plane).
Proof of identity: (1) `/tmp`-style harness compiling the old and the new `display_core.c` side by side, 4 filters x 10 sizes (1257x800 @ 352, 1280x800, 640x448, HD 2x / 3x, 1000x700, 2560x1600, 336x230 ...) x 3 frame patterns (noise, black / white / gradients, 8 px blocks), hashes of the whole output equal (120 / 120), banded rendering equal, ASan / UBSan clean; `displaytest` all ok. (2) Window screenshots (`LANDSTALKER_WINSHOT`, `SDL_VIDEODRIVER=offscreen` because the real Wayland window read back black) at frames 300 / 700 / 1100 / 1500 / 1900 / 2300 for filters 0..4 at scale FIT and FILL: old binary (`~/timebench/landstalker_pre_fx`) vs new, all 60 BMPs byte identical (frames 300 and 1100 are black in this recording; the other four differ between the filters).
Fragile: only the present thread may start a pass; the pool never joins (threads die with the process, SDL prints 'Leaked thread' at exit like it did before for the 3D room thread).

## Friday's wake-up flash in the own renderer, 2026-10-29 (Sonnet 5.5; HANDOFF 0a166)
Report: after the waterfall (room 593) Nigel wakes in bed and Friday wakes him with a blinking screen; the own renderer showed nothing. Chip picture (`LANDSTALKER_OWN=0 LANDSTALKER_OWN_NOCHIP=0`) of `rec_20261029_1729` frames 23753..23779: every other frame backdrop and colour 15 of palette line 0 are white ($EEE), the own picture (`LANDSTALKER_OWN=2`) stayed normal. Cause: `UpdateFridayAnimation_Flash $0037EE` writes CRAM 0 / 15 directly (counter $1F..$3B, even = 0x0EEE (colour), odd = 0 = black, which equals the normal colour of both) and leaves the game's palette copies alone; the own palette comes from game RAM. Fix: narrow rule at the end of `lk_o_palette`: state `$FF114B` $41 / $42 -> `lk_o_pix[0]` and `[15]` from `cram` (9 bit form, `pixel_lut[1]`). A general rule was not tried (warp pad, fades also write CRAM directly). Measuring: PNG comparison of `LANDSTALKER_SHOT` series (PIL only in `~/.landstalker_trailer/venv/bin/python`), compare mode needs `LANDSTALKER_OWN_NOCHIP=0` or the chip side is garbage and every number is meaningless (first attempt without it showed ~75000 differing px per frame). Results in HANDOFF 0a166; the WIDE 19 residual (HUD rows, right edge column, a 3x5 px block at the top right margin on odd flash frames) is margin / HUD behaviour that exists without the flash too.

## Replay divergence of the 30.10 recordings (2026-10-30, later)

HANDOFF 0a177 point 2: the four 30.10 recordings (0310, 0318, 0319, 0536) do not replay against their own `.chk`. Replay = `env $(cat rec.cfg) HOME=<empty dir> LANDSTALKER_SAVE=<copy of save> GENRECOMP_INPUTSCRIPT=rec.txt GENRECOMP_CHECKLOG=out.chk build/landstalker --headless --frames N ROM`; compare `diff <(cut -d' ' -f1-11 rec.chk) out.chk`. Findings (code 539eb72):
- **Quick load is not in the input log.** `rec_20261030_0536`: live quick load at frame 360 (Mercator room 700 -> room 454, gold 16311 -> 18871); 0319 at 341; 0318 at 424 (brute-force search for the frame). Fix: `tools/patch_qsrec.py` (`LK_QSREC v1`, `LANDSTALKER_QSLOG` / `LANDSTALKER_QSPLAY`, posted at the input poll of that frame inside `platform_quick_request`; all pad and keyboard requests pass `lk_keys_step` in `input.c`). The older `LANDSTALKER_QSREQ` posts at the frame END = one step later than a real press. Logging side not yet exercised by a live recording (headless cannot press L1).
- **Start save overwritten.** The old script let the game write into `save_<t>.srm`; 0310 and 0318 changed their save (0319, 0536, 20261029_1729 did not); their real start state is ~ `save_20261030_0319.srm`.
- **Fast forward changes the game (root cause of the rest).** `LK_SNDCLOCK v4` (`lk_ff_sndskip_n`) runs Z80 + chips only every Nth frame while fast forward is held (driven by the audio queue fill live); the game waits for the sound driver at room changes -> one GameLoop pass shifts: 0310 frame 1920 room 456 X 17.0C live vs 17.0A replay; 0536 frame 600 34.00 vs 34.02. Proof: `rec_20261029_1729` replays EXACTLY (361 checkpoints), diverges at 1440 with `LANDSTALKER_FFWD_HOLD=1 LANDSTALKER_FFWD=3`, exact again with additionally `LANDSTALKER_SNDCLOCK=0`. 0310 (no quick load) replays to 8880 instead of 1920 with FFWD_HOLD -> the user held fast forward there. The button is not recorded.
- **Options.** The `.chk` matched only with an EMPTY HOME; `--home-opts` with today's files diverges at 120 because `~/.landstalker_nativepace=1` (Native Pace did not exist before 2026-10-30 06:24).
- **Future recordings:** `tools/Landstalker_Aufnahme.sh` v2 (template; installed as `~/Landstalker_Aufnahme.sh` 2026-10-30 after the user's OK): game writes `save_<t>_ende.srm` (copied back at the end), `save_<t>.srm` stays the start state; `LANDSTALKER_QSLOG` -> `rec_<t>.qs` -> `LANDSTALKER_QSPLAY=...` in `rec_<t>.cfg`; options copied to `~/aufnahmen/opts_<t>/` (`tools/timebench.py` copies it into the replay HOME); `LANDSTALKER_SNDCLOCK=0`. The 30.10 recordings stay usable for bench self-comparisons only. Quick equip (hold R2) is still not recorded.

## Smooth Scrolling / sprint 1.5 x (2026-10-30 night; Opus 5.5)
 * Measured on `rec_20261030_2020` (LANDSTALKER_SPRINT_STEP=3, ALT=0, GENRECOMP_DUMPRANGE per frame): camera sum changes by 3 per frame, VSRAM A = floor(previous sum / 2) - 712 (FORMATS 'Scroll registers lag'); NPC screen row (+0x10) = 922 - ceil(sum / 2) in the same frame = locked to the planes; Nigel's row constant (288). -> world drawn half a line low at odd sums.
 * Fix = `tools/patch_subpix.py` (LK_SUBPIX v1 prototype, v2 option): quarter line blend of world pixels on the final 1x frame (direction from the sum's parity), Nigel (entity offset 0) / window / overlays excluded. The HD path would give a real half line but is off for the user (no tile pack) and costs much more.
 * Check: ground motion from screenshots 720..750 (SSD + parabola): Off 2 / 1 alternating, On 1.73 / 1.27; the estimator reads a synthetic 0.5 line quarter-blend shift as 0.28, so the true motion is 1.5 / 1.5. Screenshot clean. Timebench (v1 Off, v2 default On): identical except rec_20261018_1805 (options row shift) and the four 30.10 sprint recordings (not in the baseline). User: 'passt alles. einbauen' -> option default On (HANDOFF 0a204).
 * Tooling note: no numpy / PIL on the Deck; `/tmp/pngr.py` (stdlib zlib PNG reader) was enough for shot analysis.

## Sharp smooth scrolling (2026-10-31 evening; Opus 5.5)
- User: 'scharfes weiches scrollen' (MODERN_IDEAS (A2)). Built `LK_SSHARP v1` in `tools/patch_subpix.py`: `lk_ss_apply` no longer blends when the display path can shift (`lk_ssh_can`: renderer present or `LANDSTALKER_OSHOT`, not HD / 3D room, output >= 2 x 224 rows so k >= 1); it fills `lk_ssh_ok[224][448]` (world pixel nobody drew over) and sets `lk_ssh_pend` = lk_ss_frac. `lk_disp_present` turns it into `lk_ssh_d` / `lk_ssh_k` for the CPU filters (forces the CPU path at exact integer scales too: nearest there). Every kernel: per output pixel, if the mask is set at the source pixel of row y AND of row y2 = y + d * k, sample with row y2's tables (source rows, CRT Pro beam weights, scanline weight), else with row y's.
- Measured headless with the new `LANDSTALKER_OSHOT=724,725,..` (filtered output PNG, 1280x800 default, `LANDSTALKER_OSHOT_FX=n` forces a filter; prints the filter time) on rec_20261030_2020 frames 724-731, motion estimate on the output (sub-pixel least squares, Nigel's middle and the HUD excluded, script was /tmp/ssh/oshift.py): CRT Pro 1.43 / 1.57 lines per frame, CRT / Scanlines / Off / Curved the same (raw 1 / 2, blend v3 1.48 / 1.56); the remaining 0.07 line alternation = the quantisation to whole output pixels (2 px relative instead of 1.79). Hearts identical between frames. Filter time 2.6 ms with or without shift (first frame 16 ms = table build). Zoomed crops: grass / ground sharp, no seams at Nigel's outline.
- User on the Deck: 'passt. scharf ersetzt sie ganz' -> Sharp is the only visible Smooth Scrolling; no options row change; the v3 blend stays as the internal fallback / `LANDSTALKER_SS_SHARP=0`.

Schematic grid with exit direction from the door cell's floor edge + stairs = floors works (Swamp Shrine 3 floors). Room pictures via romrender.rom_planes / compose (~1 s per room, multiprocessing: 703 rooms in 2 min on the Deck).
- Runtime checked headless with `LANDSTALKER_MAPSHOT=729` on rec_20261030_2020 (room 630, Mercator, floor 2 / 3) incl. `LANDSTALKER_MAPSHOT_ALL=1`; data found next to the program (SDL_GetBasePath). Bug found before the user saw it: visited bit numbers go up to 641 (limit was 512).

## Issue #12: AppImage on Ubuntu 26.10, 'externally-managed-environment' (2026-10-31; Opus 5.5)
- Reporter's build.log: `python3 -m pip install --user --no-index --find-links <AppImage>/vendor/wheels capstone` -> error: externally-managed-environment (PEP 668, Python 3.14).
- pip 25 `commands/install.py`: the PEP 668 check runs only when installing into the current environment; `--root`, `--target`, `--prefix` skip it. firstrun.py now installs with
  `--target <DATA>/pylib` (and `--upgrade`), checks `import capstone` with PYTHONPATH=<DATA>/pylib and passes that environment to every build step. Checked on the Deck (SteamOS has
  /usr/lib/python3.13/EXTERNALLY-MANAGED as well): `pip install --target /tmp/pyl capstone==5.0.6` worked, `PYTHONPATH=/tmp/pyl python3 -c 'import capstone'` -> 5.0.6.
- The issue's own suggestions, checked on the Deck (2026-10-31): (1) venv: works on SteamOS (`python3 -m venv` + pip inside), but Debian / Ubuntu ship `ensurepip` only in the
  separate python3-venv package ('ensurepip is not available'), so a venv needs an extra system package on exactly the reporter's distribution; (2) `--user --break-system-packages`:
  installs (the import test was meaningless here: SteamOS already has capstone in /usr/lib/python3.13/site-packages), but writes into the player's ~/.local for every python
  program (what PEP 668 protects) and pip < 23.0.1 (e.g. Ubuntu 22.04's 22.0.2) rejects the unknown flag = a second code path. Kept `--target` (no system package, no flag, any pip
  that has --target, own folder that is removed with our data).

## VDP data path steps 3.0 - 3.2 (2026-11-01; Opus 5.5)
Goal (SOUND_PLAN section 12): GPGX's `vdp_ctrl.c` decode replaced by `tools/lkvdp.c` (#included into vdp_ctrl.c by `tools/patch_vdpport.py`, marker `LK_VDPPORT v3`), opt-in `LANDSTALKER_VDP=own|check` until step 3.5.
- **3.0 count** (`tools/patch_vdpcount.py`, `LK_VDPCOUNT v1`, `LANDSTALKER_VDPCOUNT=file`, `tools/vdpcount_sum.py`): over 74 runs the game uses control words (15.9 M + 4.3 M), register writes, data writes to VRAM / CRAM / VSRAM (+ 365 to the invalid code 2), DMA 68K->VDP from work RAM and ROM, fill (always started by the next data write); never data reads, copy, DMA from io, mode 4, Z80 side, writes while a DMA is busy, `vdp_68k_irq_ack` (our `recomp_m68k_interrupt` acknowledges).
- **3.1 data port write** (`lkv_bus_w`): decode, byte swap, store into `lk_vm` / `lk_cm` / `lk_vsm`, widescreen hooks, auto increment; GPGX's FIFO (its fill and our FIFO timing read it), address, chip memories, SAT cache, pattern cache, palette fed by `lkv_chip_feed` until 3.5.
- **3.2a control port** (`lkv_ctrl_w`): command latch + own register file `lkv.reg[]` + DMA start; pending is also cleared by data writes and status reads (`lk_vdp_status_ack`); GPGX's DMA engine rewrites registers 19..22 at the end of a DMA (`lkv_pull_dmaregs`).
- **3.2b register side effects** (`lkv_reg_w`): everything of GPGX's mode 5 `vdp_reg_w`; fallback to GPGX for mode 4, the mode 4 -> 5 switch and register 8 (4 writes per run at boot). The IRQ line it sets (`m68k.int_level`) is not read by our runtime (interrupts are delivered by bus.c), kept for parity.
- **Proof:** timebench (re-baselined this session with `~/timebench/bin_pre31`, 74 runs) own IDENTICAL 74 / 74 after every step; check mode 0 mismatches (265 M data words, 20.3 M control words, 11.6 M register writes, 0 sync misses); screenshots gpgx vs own 266 / 266 identical.
- **3.3 DMA engine** (`lkv_dma_update`, `LK_VDPPORT v4`): bookkeeping + transfer ours (timing was already ours: `lk_v_dma_calc`); check = prediction from the state before + GPGX's chunk nested; 1.43 M chunks 0 mismatches, own IDENTICAL 74 / 74.
- **Next:** 3.4 data port wrapper + reset, 3.5 default on + poison of the chip memories, then drop `lkv_chip_feed` and the renderer upkeep.

## VDP data path steps 3.3 - 3.5 + system start 4.1 - 4.3 (2026-11-01; Opus 5.5)
- **3.3** DMA engine ours (`LK_VDPPORT v4`, `lkv_dma_update`): own IDENTICAL 74 / 74, check 1.43 M chunks 0 mismatches, screenshots 266 / 266 (commit a78a2fd).
- **3.4** data port write wrapper ours (`LK_VDPPORT v5`, `lkv_data_w`): own IDENTICAL 74 / 74, check 6 M data writes 0 mismatches (505262c). The session broke off after 3.4; recovered from the working tree + `~/timebench/vdp34_*` / `vdp35_*` logs.
- **3.5** default ours (`LK_VDPPORT v6`, 8bf4578). First runs: default 1 RAM diff (rec_20261012_2106), poison 21 time trace diffs (RAM / WAV equal).
  - Poison: `--keep-traces` of rec_20261012_2148 normal vs `LANDSTALKER_OWN_POISON=1`: only 2 lines `S` (status read) 520 vs 552 = bit 5 (sprite collision). Cause: GPGX's `parse_satb_m5` / `_im2` read name / attribute + x position from the chip VRAM (`p[link + 2]`, `p[link + 3]`); the collision bit comes from GPGX's sprite renderer. Fix: read `lk_vm` in own mode (`lk_vdp_ownmem`, patched into vdp_render.c by patch_vdpport.py).
  - RAM diff: dumps of own vs gpgx mode differed from frame 30800 at one VRAM word $9780 (ours 0x00F1, chip 0x4F00). `LANDSTALKER_OWN_VMDIFF=1` showed the mirror vs chip split at frame 30772 in BOTH modes = old bug of the mirror. Cause: VRAM fill (and copy) work on the ADJACENT byte (`WRITE_BYTE(vram, addr ^ 1)`); our big endian copy wrote `lk_vm[addr]`. Fixed in patch_vdpmirror.py (fill + copy), lkvdp.c lkv_fill and the fill check of the DMA compare (it had predicted the same wrong byte).
  - Final: timebench default / poison / gpgx / check IDENTICAL 74 / 74; check 265 M words, 20.3 M ctrl, 11.6 M regw, 1.43 M DMA, 6 M data writes, 0 mismatches; `LANDSTALKER_SHOT` gpgx vs own intro (every 60 frames) + rec_20261012_2106 30000-31200 (every 20) 259 / 259 identical.
- **4.1** chip reset ours, opt-in `LANDSTALKER_BOOT=own|check` (`LK_VDPPORT v7`, `lkv_reset`, 1e145b4): check compares every variable vdp_reset writes (73 231 bytes) 0 mismatches; timebench BOOT=own + default IDENTICAL.
- **4.2** vdp_init (`LK_VDPPORT v8`) + gen_reset / io_reset (`tools/lkboot.c`, `tools/patch_boot.py`, `LK_BOOT v1`, 63a5254): check 84 670 bytes 0 mismatches; timebench IDENTICAL.
- **4.3** gen_init memory maps + plain cartridge init (Landstalker only; `LK_BOOT v2`, fe4d007): check 81 121 bytes + ROM area hash 0 mismatches; timebench IDENTICAL.
- Pitfall: `timebench.py --out FILE` and `LANDSTALKER_VDP_CHECKLOG=FILE` must be different files (the --out file overwrote the check counters once).
- Not done yet: user check on the Deck (3.5 default on; optionally `LANDSTALKER_BOOT=own`); 4.4 = BOOT default ours after the user's OK; zig cc Windows compile of the new code (SOUND_PLAN acceptance).

## System start 4.4, renderer speed-ups, old renderer off 5.1 (2026-11-01 evening / night; Opus 5.5)
- **4.4** (b62e5e3): user confirmed 3.5 on the Deck ('passt') -> `LANDSTALKER_BOOT` default ours (`LK_VDPPORT v9`, `LK_BOOT v3`); timebench default + BOOT=gpgx IDENTICAL; user then confirmed the own start too ('passt weiter').
- **Profile** (`perf record -g`, rec_20261029_1729, 6000 frames, headless): own renderer ~60 %; GPGX renderer leftovers ~4-5 % (remap_line, parse_satb_m5, render_line, render_bg via the text box); real waste in our code: `lk_hd_fxany() || lk_r3d_on()` asked per PIXEL in lk_own_row (14 %), `lk_o_init` read ~10 env variables per call = per line (2.5 %).
- **Speed-up 1** (8f03d0b): both hoisted (once per line / once at start). **Speed-up 2** (50f0b2c): `lk_o_fetch` always_inline. No gain (dropped): inlining lk_scr_ci / lk_scr_cell / lk_o_win, `-march=native` (build is -O3 already).
- **CPU numbers** (user time, best of 3, measured INTERLEAVED because the Deck drifts +- 1 s): bin_44 23.7 s, after speed-up 1 18.6 s, after speed-up 2 17.5 s, after 5.1 16.5 s = ~30 % less in total. Every step: screenshots vs before identical (intro every 60 frames + rec_20261029_1729 every 300 frames, 342 / 342), timebench IDENTICAL 74 / 74.
- **5.1** (41fc583, `LK_CHIPLINE v2`, `tools/patch_chipline.py`, default; rollback `LANDSTALKER_CHIPLINE=1`): GPGX's render_line extras (wide margins, text box composition, blanking) and remap_line's chip buffer copy skipped where our renderer draws (own On, NOCHIP, no HD pack). Proof: magenta mark (`LANDSTALKER_CHIPLINE_MARK=1`) screenshots 1393 / 1393 (74 recordings, every 300 frames) + 1092 / 1092 (8 Map Behind Text recordings, every 20 frames; `LANDSTALKER_TBOX_DEBUG` counted >16 000 frames with the box over the map) identical; sanity: `LANDSTALKER_OWN_SCREENS=0` makes the title magenta (89 152 px). Timebench IDENTICAL incl. the time trace: the sprite collision bit 5 is never set in normal play (the earlier poison trace diff came from garbage objects in GPGX's sprite list). User: 'sieht alles gut aus'.
- **5.2 RESULT:** the per-line chain walk cost as much as GPGX's parse (1.96 vs 1.73 % in perf, 0.3 s slower) -> per-line count table rebuilt only on SAT cache / width change (`lk_sat_dirty`). Full check 90 272 313 parses, 3 124 overflows, 0 mismatches; timebench default / check / rollback IDENTICAL. Original note: **5.2** (in progress at the time of writing): GPGX's `parse_satb_m5` reduced to its only used effect, the sprite overflow status bit 6, computed by `lk_spr_ovf` from our SAT cache `lk_sat16` (lkvdp.c, `LK_VDPPORT v10`; written at the same points as GPGX's sat[]: chip feed, fill, lk_vram_put_word); `LK_CHIPLINE v3`, `LANDSTALKER_SPRSTAT=own|gpgx|check` (+ `LANDSTALKER_SPRSTAT_LOG=file`). First check run rec_20261029_1729 6000 frames: 1 290 662 parses, 0 mismatches.
- Pitfall: a Python patch tool that builds C with `%` formatting: inside an r''' replacement the format operator must stay a single `%` (only the C text gets `%%`).
- **5.3** (2026-11-02, `LK_CHIPLINE v5` + `LK_VDPPORT v11`, default; rollback `LANDSTALKER_CHIPFEED=1`): profile first (perf rec_20261029_1729 6000 frames): GPGX leftovers only remap_line 0.9 % + render_line 0.6 %, the chip feed itself < 0.2 %; getenv 0.7 % (callers found with `perf record --call-graph dwarf` + `perf script`, then objdump at the call offset -> the string = LANDSTALKER_SPRINT_STEP). Design: lazy chip memories (lk_chip_feed / lk_chip_stale / lk_chip_resync); decision once per line at the start of render_line (off = own VDP path + CHIPLINE off + own sprite status). Readers outside render_line that would see stale data: vdp_68k / z80 data read (VRAM / VSRAM) and vdp_dma_copy -> resync first; lk_vram_put_word (wide strips) gated too. Result: timebench default / poison / rollback / check IDENTICAL 74 / 74 to step 5.2 (ss52c_def); check (`LANDSTALKER_CHIPFEED=check`, the image rebuilt from our memories compared with the fed chip memories at line 0 of every frame) 426 976 compares, 0 mismatch bytes; poison (`LANDSTALKER_CHIPFEED_POISON=1`: chip VRAM / SAT cache / VSRAM + pattern cache = garbage at line 0 of every frame while not fed) 106.6 M lines not fed, 0 resyncs needed, screenshots 1394 / 1394 identical (74 recordings every 300 frames). CPU: feed off vs `LANDSTALKER_CHIPFEED=1` no measurable difference (15.08 vs 15.06 s, the feed already cost < 0.2 %); the gain is the clean cut. ALSO: two env reads per call removed (perf: getenv 0.7 %): `patch_sprint.py` LANDSTALKER_SPRINT_STEP read before the `done` check, `patch_wide.py` lk_strip_extend LANDSTALKER_TALL_DEBUG before the address test -> 15.38 -> 15.08 s (rec_20261029_1729, 6000 frames, best of 3 interleaved, ~2 %). Note: timebench deletes its work dirs at the end -> check / poison counters only via `LANDSTALKER_CHIPFEED_LOG=<file>`.

## Castle torches and indoor shadows (2026-11-05, Deck, Opus 5.5)
- User screenshots of room 55 (MERCATOR_CASTLE_ENTRANCE_HALLWAY): a lit BOX around each standing torch, castle too dark, then a huge shadow block, frayed edges, jagged knight shadows.
- DBG=1 now prints each light's flame entry: all castle standing torches are `$098AAA` (Tileset02Anim01). Lights of room 55: (485,399,65), (792,248,56), (693,399,65); none inside a solid.
- Box cause: the indoor view ray (`lk_lt_ray`) put the wall / floor pixels behind a torch pillar on the pillar's 16 x 16 collision column (cells (29,24) / (42,24), height 4) right under the flame.
  Fix `lk_lt_thin`: layer B pixels pass through a column that CARRIES a flame (light within one cell, z >= top - 8); column = <= 4 cells of one height and the same blocked
  flag, void / higher walls beside it allowed (room 87: walkable pillar cells of height 5 in front of a blocked wall of height 5). First try without the flame
  condition made the back wall pieces of row 16 (height 96, void behind) 'thin' = the huge wrong dark wall (probe: L0 term 63 -> 0, face 3 -> face 1).
- A flame's own pillar shaded the room (light 1 unit above the column top): `lk_lt_solid` uses the floor of thin columns. The far right shadow block of room 55 is real:
  an off-screen standing torch in an alcove (cell 49,15, `3#` beside walls of height 6) shines out only through its opening.
- Soft edges: indoor march takes min(IPEN 4 * clearance / distance); figure shadows from torches = share of the 3 x 3 sprite pixels; torches as 2-point area lights

## Boot Colors option + release v1.8.0 (2026-11-04 night, Deck, Opus 5.5)
- Request (user): boots should show on Nigel like sword / armour (palette swap in the original), optional under Graphics. Finding: the boots use line 2 entries 8 / 3 / 4 / 5, the same browns as hair, gloves and shoulder plates (`DefaultPlayerPal $00901C`), so only a per pixel recolour works (FORMATS 'Nigel's boots').
- Method: Python reference first (`tools/bootcol_check.py`: decode all 72 poses of sprite graphic 0, 4-connected brown areas, lowest 30 % rule), numbered pose sheets reviewed by the user (fixes: bed picture out, gloves in the lying poses, raised sole rims 6 / 28, swing cuff 58 + back boot 54, scabbard ends 6 / 28 / 34 / 41 / 45 / 61 as never-boot boxes, small pieces at the scabbard highlight / sword colours excluded). SEEDS / CUT / BOOTCOL live only in that tool; `tools/patch_bootcol.py` writes the C tables from it.
- C side (`LK_BOOTCOL v2`): mask per pose in tile space (cache per animation / frame), boot pixels stamped while `lk_o_sprpiece` draws Nigel (slot 0, graphic 0), tinted before Lighting at the pixel's current brightness. Screens (file select): pose found by its VRAM tiles (palette line 2 sprites, lowest tile = start, FNV hash + memcmp against every pose over the pose's OWN tile count), boot pixels flagged per line in the screen renderer.
- Checks: `LANDSTALKER_BOOTCOL_DBG=1` + `bootcol_check.py cmp ROM LOG`: game = reference on all 42 poses of massan.txt after every change; file select: `docs/replays/file_select_slot1_slash.txt` (walk 0x0C + swing 0x3C poses found; save 1 shows Healing boots) and `docs/replays/file_select_slot_switch.txt`.
- Bug (user): slot change and back -> brown boots while walking. Cause: after the swing the sprite table keeps stale pieces (31 instead of 24 tiles), the first version required the total tile count to equal the pose's. Fix: compare each pose over its own tiles from the start tile.
- Colours: icon colours too loud (user) -> muted steps B / C / D (`~/Mockups/stiefel_gedaempft*.png`), user chose C then B, Healing green closer to its icon (option 2).
- Release v1.8.0: timebench vs `baseline_lights2026-11-03.txt` 73 / 74 identical (+ `rec_20261022_0706` = menu recording, Graphics page one row longer), 8 new recordings chk=equal, new baseline `~/timebench/baseline_v1.8.0.txt`; zig cc windows / linux compile of vdp_render.c, platform_sdl.c, func_table.c clean (note: `zig cc -fsyntax-only` reports a bogus `FileNotFound`, use `-c -o`); CI run 41924391744; AppImage first run (clean HOME) 2 min 13 s, built game vs baseline IDENTICAL 82 / 82.

## Modern effects: ambient particles, enemy dissolve (2026-11-05 night, Deck, Opus 5.5)

- Enemy health +$3E (watched per slot in the debug build): after the death frame it is <= 0 and then COUNTS UP by 1 per frame (death animation / drop timer); the death frame itself already has +$0C cleared to $01, so the enemy test (+$0C & $82 = $80) must look at the PREVIOUS frame. On room load +$3E goes 1024 -> 0 for non-enemy slots (3899 such cases in the recording), ignored by the enemy bit test.
- Replay `rec_20261105_2125` with `LANDSTALKER_DISSOLVE_DBG=1`: bursts at frames 1401, 1583, 1589, 1869 with 229..1026 sprite pixels (spawn capped at ~250 per enemy); preview `~/Mockups/gegner_zerfallen.mp4`.

- Drop landing check (composite method): diff the frames of a 5 s run with particles ON against `LANDSTALKER_AMBIENT=0`, accumulate the differing pixels over all frames into one picture; the landing points showed drops ended on wall / pillar / box tops (first version took every up-facing light face as floor) and began inside the picture. Fix: walkable floor only (height map word not $4000, no $60 flags, z = 16 * height) and spawn z from `lk_lt_vmin` (above the picture top); composite checked again (`~/Mockups/tropfen_von_oben.png`).

## Session 2026-11-06 GPU step 2 measurement + GPU lighting GL1 (Deck, Opus 5.5)

- Effect cost, windowed runs (massan prefix, `LANDSTALKER_WARP=610:12.08,20.04`, `LANDSTALKER_DAYTIME=12`, 2700 frames, bash `time` user + sys): default 27.9 s; Clouds 0 -> 27.7, Water 0 -> 27.3, Lighting 0 -> 17.9, Day / Night 0 -> 27.8, GPU 0 -> 116.5 (user's CRT Pro); room 403 default 29.9, Haze 0 -> 27.4. Note: windowed runs read the LIVE controller too (the user's inputs went into a run).
- perf (`perf record -F 999 -g`, headless, works without root on the Deck): 610 lk_own_row 34 %, lk_lt_pix 23 %, lk_lt_figdir 8 %, lk_lt_block 3.4 %, lk_lt_sunvis 2.5 %; 177 lk_lt_pix 30 %, lk_own_row 28 %, lk_lt_voidglow 6.7 %, lk_lt_block 3.6 %, lk_lt_blockcalc 2.5 %.
- GL1 harness (`LK_GPULT v1`): hidden window runs first hung = the focus pause (`focus pause: window lost focus`), not vsync: `LANDSTALKER_NOFOCUSPAUSE=1`. `s_frame_no` is incremented BEFORE `lk_disp_present`: the capture of frame N is checked at present with frame N + 1. Results: 610 noon / 17 / 21 h and 177 noon: 71..90 M pixels each exact; time-lapse (`LANDSTALKER_TIMELAPSE=1`, start 6 h, 4500 frames) 610 277 M and 630 288 M exact (figsun pixels 0.1 M / 2.5 M excluded). The warp spot 610:12.08,20.04 puts Nigel into a house wall (no figure sun shadow there). Off path: `LANDSTALKER_SHOT` + `--ram-dump` in 177 identical to the binary before.
- GL2 (`LK_GPULT v2`, 2100 frames each, massan prefix): 177 at 21 h 127 M pixels (93 off by 1); 610 at 21 h 131 M (45); 171 noon (key light, 3 shafts) 131 M (44; excluded figshadow 179 k, pool shade 210 k, motes 37 k); 403 lava 131 M (84); 177 with `LANDSTALKER_LIGHTING_SWORD=1` 127 M (94); 0 pixels off by more than 1 everywhere. Off by 1 = GPU float rounding (sqrt / fused multiply-add) at the int truncation.
- GL3 (`LK_GPULT v3`, `LANDSTALKER_LT_SHHALF=0`): 171 134 M pixels (48 off by 1; with figshadow 179 k, pool shade 210 k, motes 37 k, all exact), 177 at 21 h 134 M (107; flame shadow 6.3 M), Gumi 425 at 22 h 134 M (55; particles 15 k, figshadow 104 k), Mercator 630 time-lapse 4000 frames 321 M (51; figshadow 2.3 M); 0 worse. With the default SHHALF=1 (177 at 21 h, 4000 frames): 1.33 M of 321 M pixels worse (0.4 %), all at figure flame shadow edges (flag 16) = the CPU shortcut (odd x copies the left neighbour's shadow share). `lrintf` in GLSL 1.20: floor(v + 0.5) with the tie to even; C `(int)` of negative values = truncation (particles mix `(cr - r) * a`).
- GL4 (`LK_GPULT v4`): first version lit the Modern HUD pixels not at all -> soft HUD edges (hearts outline, gold, moon) brighter than the CPU path at night (diff picture showed the HUD outlines); fix: the HUD is a 'mix after the light' like the window glass (max diff Ryuma 21 h 55 -> 4). Speed: the emissive chain and `lk_mh_pix` first run on the pixel itself and only on black / white when they change it (each of them 3 x per lit pixel cost ~1 k perf samples); row position + frame stamps once per line (`lk_gl_line`), mixes in a list reset at the next frame. perf Ryuma noon 2700 frames: CPU lighting 33 K samples (lk_own_row 11.9 K, lk_lt_pix 7.0 K, lk_lt_figdir 2.6 K, lk_lt_block 1.2 K) -> GPU lighting 26 K (lk_own_row 14.0 K). Windowed process CPU time (hidden window): 610 29.6 -> 25.8 s, 177 29.4 -> 22.6 / 23.9 s (run-to-run noise ~1 s), 171 25.6 -> 24.5 s. Tools: `/tmp`-only PNG diff script (pure Python, no PIL / numpy on the Deck).

## Session 2026-11-07: STEP 4 stage 4a, GPU picture composition harness (Deck, Opus 5.5)
- perf (`perf record -F 999 -g` through a wrapper as the BIN argument of `tools/gpucmp.sh`, rec_20261106_1322, 4440 frames, Light Engine GPU): `lk_own_row` 46.6 % self (helpers inlined: lk_o_fetch, the classify), then lk_mh_pix 2.5 %, lkym_update 2.1 %, lk_wr_pix 1.7 %, lk_o_win 1.6 %, lk_scr_row 1.3 %. The hot spots inside lk_own_row are spread (no single line > 6 % of it): memory loads of the per-pixel state; a per-pixel `platform_frame_number()` call sits in lk_gl_classify's on-demand block check (cheap, but per pixel).
- Harness `tools/patch_gpucomp.py` (LK_GPUCOMP v1): CPU records per line (lk_gc_linebegin, called next to the other *_linebegin of lk_own_row) and per pixel (after the lk_vh history line: pick | src << 6 | black, colour); the GPU pass runs in lk_gpu_present before lk_glt_pass(0) (GPU Filters must be On), GLSL 1.20 like the other passes, integers packed in RGBA8 / luminance textures, two draws (colour / meta) + glReadPixels. Data: layers 304 x 304 RGBA (A rows 0..151, B 152..303; r / g = tile word, b = present bit), tiles 1024 x 72 luminance (0x12000 bytes), sprites 448 x 224 LA (lk_o_spr as is), window 320 x 224 LA (wp + 1), palette 64 x 224 BGRA (each line's own palette), rows 8 x 224 RGBA.
- Results (EXTRA="LANDSTALKER_GPUCOMP=2" tools/gpucmp.sh REC OUT): rec_20261106_1322 288.7 M pixels, 1305 264.6 M, 1917 240.6 M, 2031 144.3 M, rec_20261105_2125 938.2 M: all exact, 0 meta / 0 colour differences (sources: backdrop, sprite, A, B, window all present; black 0 because Full Height + Modern HUD). 1305 with LANDSTALKER_TALL=0 LANDSTALKER_HUD=0 LANDSTALKER_TBOX=0 (a local gpucmp copy that puts EXTRA AFTER the recording's cfg variables, which otherwise win): 264.6 M exact incl. 48 M black and 26 M window pixels. Warp pad bands: covered by construction (the line's palette is the CPU's choice), no recording with a warp pad was replayed.
- Off path: `LANDSTALKER_PRESENT_SHOT` frames 600..4200 of rec_20261106_1322, old binary vs new: 7 / 7 byte-identical.
- Effect use per line (harness counter, 4.6 M lines): Modern HUD 100 %, GPU light 100 %, water 75 %, boots 75 %, clouds 47 %, sub-pixel 35 %, lit windows 6 %, sword fx 2 %, dissolve 0.4 %, haze 0 -> stage order in RENDERER_IDEAS 'STEP 4 STAGE PLAN'.
- **Stage 4b (LK_GPUCOMP v2, same session):** GPU side = boot colours (sprite word bit 11 from lk_bc_st == lk_bc_stamp, lk_bc_tint with the line's 4 boot / 4 base colours), lk_gl_classify (figures via uniforms, lk_lt_figat box), lk_dn_winpix as mix after the light (glass masks lk_dn_wm as uniforms, w per line), lk_wr_pix (tile class per tile: animated water group / static water tile from lk_wr_ts or the same 97 % rule without filling the cache; lk_wr_pal / lk_wr_idx; tint; reflection lk_wr_buf + per-line ripple table from the CPU's own sinf / float maths), lk_cs_pix (128 x 128 texture, ox >> 3 / oy >> 3 / kf), Modern HUD (lk_mh_a / lk_mh_c: class 0 -> colour, else p0 / p1 when it changes the colour). C integer division towards zero in GLSL 1.20 = `a >= 0 ? floor(a / b) : -floor(-a / b)`; no `%` / uniform 1fv / 2fv loaders in this file: masks and figures go as vec4 arrays.

## Session 2026-11-08 evening: front void + soft back wall key shadow (Deck, Opus 5.5)
Task: HANDOFF -132 item 1, cave floor rim bands 246 / 250 / 242 / 162. Measurement (room 250): `tools/lightedges.py` shots, the ROM picture located in the shot by matching equal-neighbour patterns (palette independent; shot pixel (0,0) = U 1005 V 272, Nigel at U 1184 = shot x 179 = probe x 160: `LANDSTALKER_LIGHTING_PROBE` x is the shot x - WIDE 19, and only some pixels print), then U / V -> world at the floor height: the drawn floor ends exactly at the room edge (cell 32 end = 528), the hard step lies at wy ~507 ON the last rows. Height words: floor 0x0200 cells 19..32, everything else 0x4000.
Cause: `LANDSTALKER_LT_KEY=0` removed the hard step (AO only a soft falloff remained), `LANDSTALKER_LT_KBW=0` reduced it; `lk_lt_aoh2` returns 32767 (wall) for a void cell with `lk_lt_vw` bit 0 when marching -x; bit 0 was set for every void cell with no room cell within 8 at -x, so row y = 33 IN FRONT of the room was a wall for the key light (sd = (-0.45, +0.45, 0.77)).
Fix 1 (`lk_lt_vwnear`): bit 0 / 1 also needs a room cell within 8 at +x / +y. Fix 2 (`lk_lt_kbwsoft`): the remaining back wall shadow line in 250 was knife-sharp because a back wall was 1e9 high below zref + kbw and the march returned at z > wt (= zref + kbw when ztop is lower) before any clearance was measured; now hh = zref + kbw and the exit test is `kpen * (z - wt) >= res * t * hl` (the sun march's rule; r grows with t because the start lies below wt). First try of fix 2 without the exit change did NOTHING (same 253 edges with KBWSOFT=0 / 1): check that a softening change can actually be reached before measuring.
Evidence: lightedges 652 indoor rooms before (binary copy ~/lktest/landstalker_before) / after: 78083 -> 57268 edge px, 521 rooms changed; outdoor 488 / 632 / 486 / 524 / 638 pixel-identical (638: 5 px dust); GL5 compare (gpucmp.sh rec_20261103_1803 + warps 250 / 242 / 246, GL5=0 DAYNIGHT=0 EVERY=20): key exact 357936, worse 0. Pitfall: a build that failed in the C compiler still gave `verify_build.sh` '102 ok, Stand ist aktuell' (it checks the patch markers in the sources, not the binary): check `Build exit` and the binary's time.

Fix `lk_lt_kslant` (fade over ndl -0.25 .. +0.25 for slanted codes). ## Real 3D light shapes stage 1 + 2 (2026-11-08 late)
Code: `tools/patch_lights.py` marker LK_LTMODEL v1: `mesh_tris()` reads every OBJ of a `mesh` op into triangles with smooth normals (faces within 35 deg averaged, back-facing triangles get flipped normals) -> C tables lk_lt_mtri%d / lk_lt_mtris / lk_lt_mtrin; indoors (no sun) `lk_lt_finebuild` marks each sub column a model stamps as model owned (lk_lt_mown), ground below the model lk_lt_mg = the op's "floor" if given (ship: sea -22) else min(ground, model bottom) (NOT the game's cell height: under the ship that is the DECK = collision), model bottom lk_lt_mb (bottom above mg + 4 = floating part, air below). `tools/patch_gpulight.py` marker LK_GLMODEL v1: fine texture got a 5th plane (296 x 1480; rows 1184..1479: r owned, g bottom - ground clamped 0..255, b/a ground); `lk_gl_mlist()` = the room's model triangles; at a new room world (lk_g5_bind) `lk_gm_render()` rasterizes them into a MODEL BUFFER (2048 x 1024 texture, left half, FBO + depth renderbuffer, texture unit 11, uniforms mdl / uMo / uMdl): picture space U = x - y + 1184, V = (x + y)/2 - z, orthographic along the view ray (1,1,1), depth = -z, texel z*8+32768 (r/g) + octahedral normal (b/a). The indoor view ray returns the model surface when reached before any field hit: face 1 if normal z > 0.94 else NEW FACE CODE 20 with the real normal (globals gN / gMB; FNX/FNY/new FNZ read gN for code 20; room A gets the nearest old code via fclassic() so the light pass and CPU readers never see 20). STAGE 2 (key light): SHADOW MAP of the models along lk_lt_sd (right half of the same texture; s = p.e1, t = p.e2, depth = p.d; up to 4 texels per unit; redrawn on world or direction change; uniforms uSm / uE1 / uE2); keyShare = field march WITHOUT the model's own sub columns / slabs / halo (global gKM) + smvis() (blocker search 5x5, then 7x7 filter, width = blocker distance * horizontal part of d / kpen, bias 1.5). AO with models: floating model sub columns are air down to the ground (aoh2), near a model the 4 sub column heights are interpolated (aohm), the ground below a model counts like water for the depth darkening (the ship's deck cells gave 16 unit stairs on the sea). Ship recipe `remake/recipes/proxy_ship_493.py`: side rail logs over the tapering bow (y 744..784) now sit on the deck (z 32..44), the old boxes down to z 0 stuck out of the slanted bow side = vertical light streak ('harte Kanten'); `data/proxies/ship_493.obj` rebuilt (118 faces). 
Evidence (headless): rooms without models pixel identical before / after (presented GPU pictures, warps 250 / 600 / 632 / 486, LANDSTALKER_DAYNIGHT=0); ship 493 (warp 493:39.08,42.08,0010) and 494: dark phantom columns on the sea left of the bow gone, sawtooth hull key shadow on the sea soft, mast lit as a round body, no blocky AO on the lower hull; GPU block time not measurably different. Test aids: LANDSTALKER_LT_MODEL=0 (off), LANDSTALKER_LT_MODELSM=0 (stage 1 only), LANDSTALKER_GL5_MODELDBG=1/2/3..9. 
Measuring method: LANDSTALKER_GL5_MODELDBG=2 prints per computed block pixel 'gm: U V face key ao z km' (room A + B planes); a stdlib script turns two logs (model on / off) into per-area diffs and grey maps (key share | AO | face) in room picture space. =3..9 make aoP return markers / the horizon part / the strongest occluder height, x, y, z (this found that the occluder was the deck cell height used as ground). =1 prints the model buffer / shadow map sizes. Headless shots: hidden window (LANDSTALKER_HIDDEN=1 LANDSTALKER_NOFOCUSPAUSE=1) + LANDSTALKER_PRESENT_SHOT, warp after the rec_20261103_1803 prefix (script kept locally in ~/lktest/m3d/shot.sh, not in git).
Failed tries: (1) ground below the model from the game's cell height (= the ship's deck): AO still saw the hull as columns; fixed with the op "floor". (2) An AO override for all model side faces removed the wanted contact shadow at wall feet; dropped. (3) The AO stairs on the sea came from the depth darkening (deck cells counted as shallow ground), not from the heights; fixed by treating the ground below a model like water.

**Outdoor path + opt-in (dev b5b2919).** Tries: (1) model buffer for layer A only in the uRayF branch: walls drawn in layer B lay under their own model and were shadowed by it (room 636 wall tops sun 208 -> 0); fixed by taking both layers. (2) All meshes as real surfaces (LANDSTALKER_LT_MODELALL=1): room 636 arch_636 has hip boxes standing in for pine clumps and tower half cylinders the outdoor carve trims, so they cast phantom shadows (blue strip on the grass ledge); fixed by opt-in per mesh op ("surface": true, ship 493..497 only). (3) Plain 1.5 bias in the shadow map filter let wide taps count the own flat / slanted surface as blocker; fixed by receiver plane bias in smvis2 (ship < 13 levels, 4087 px). Maps: 3072 x 1024 texture (model buffer | map 0 | map 1), 36 redraws in a 486 time-lapse. Arcade 486 as surface: lighter side penumbra at wall feet, left off. Next: user test of the ship; then per outdoor model verify (remake/tools/proxy_bgtest.py, remake-grade recipe without stand-in boxes) and mark "surface": true, 486 first with a user eye check (LANDSTALKER_LT_MODELALL=1 vs default); flame shadows from models still open.

## Session 2026-11-08 night .. 2026-11-09: whole rooms as 3D models, church 632, trees as surfaces, entry stutter (Deck, Opus 5.5 + 1 Sonnet scan agent)
Plan LIGHT_PROXY_PLAN 'WHOLE ROOMS AS 3D MODELS'; HANDOFF 0a342 .. 0a352; dev 1110500 .. 43a277f.
**Room box model (LK_LTROOM v1 / LK_GLROOM v1).** The light's own height field (indoors lk_lt_ifh after every rule; outdoors the heights of the room's own view ray: cell ray lk_lt_top / fine ray lk_lt_vg) becomes greedy box quads at room load (0.8 ms CPU), drawn into the GPU model buffer (now 2048 x 2048, texture 4096 x 2048 with the two 1024 shadow map slots). Ownership kinds in lk_lt_mown / fine plane 4: 1 hand model, 2 generated, 3 field answers (thin pillars, outdoor objects). Every ray decides model vs field BEFORE the march (gdec). Edge conventions copied from the field: geometry nudged -0.02 / -0.01, back faces discarded in the view buffer, vertical corner lines (x - y multiple of 4) and wall-foot ties answered by the field, generated surfaces marked z*8+49152 (hand models z*8+32768, objects z*8+16384). Proof: per-pixel 'gm:' dumps (LANDSTALKER_GL5_MODELDBG=2, =1 every pixel with LANDSTALKER_GL5_MODELDBGALL=1) and GPU sweeps (~/lktest/rm/sweep.py): 294 rooms pixel-identical -> default ON. Dropped: the 'nearest whole z' on walls (2 pixel band on room 680's stair, user saw it). Bisect switches: LANDSTALKER_LT_ROOMDRAW=0, _ROOMOWN=0, LANDSTALKER_GL5_MODELDBG=20, LANDSTALKER_LT_IFORCE=1 (room 640 = a SUN room; the fine outdoor ray != the cell ray).
**Back face shadow maps.** Dark 1 px lines along hand-model edges = PCF taps catching the neighbouring face (bench: room boxes in the shadow map, LANDSTALKER_LT_ROOMSM=1, against the exact field march; tunables LANDSTALKER_LT_SMO only helped partly). Fix: only light-averted triangles in the shadow maps (gl_FrontFacing; all proxy OBJs outward CCW: every directed edge has its twin, volume > 0). Church corner 89 -> 208, extra dark pixels vs field 249 -> 14.
**Church 632 re-measured** with remake/tools/fit_foot.py (wall foot on known ground fixes x / y; longest run per column; --eave), the grey roof terraces and the gable profile: hall x 238..378 y 797..922 eave 60 hip 107.5; house x 240..319 y 928..992 eave 64 terrace 79.5 (hip:17); cross gable x 340..394.5 y 823..890 eave 60 ridge 93.5 (old recipe 5..6 units off). Surface fixes: clean-up of the cells a surface model stands in + raised collision cells of the op rectangle (yellow house collision reaches row 62; LANDSTALKER_LT_CPKDUMP=x,y showed its side record), turned-away wall rule model-aware, near-axis model walls -> classic face codes 2 / 3 (mfc). Remaining look difference: Bounce Light treats the 45 deg roof as a +y wall (door wall a bit darker, identical with LANDSTALKER_BOUNCE=0).
**Trees / bushes as surfaces (LK_LTOBJ v1 / LK_GLOBJ v1).** Meshes of revolution of the field's own shapes (bush ellipsoid on stem, 6-tier pine, lathe profile), layer A pixels only, kind 1 ownership, back face shadow maps. Speed: GPU vertex buffers (lk_gm_bindmesh), shadow-map-only redraws on sun steps with slot reuse (uSmSl), bbox extents, 8 / 12 segments: densest forest 561 (645 objects, 145k triangles) sun step ~1.2 ms. 155-room outdoor sweep clean (one run's black batches = hidden-window display hiccup; re-run clean).
**Entry stutter (user recording rec_20261109_0502).** Church entry 0 / 77 / 92..124 ms (old / room model / + models); GPU entry window 1.0 -> 3.3 ms per frame. Causes: per-sample model tests in every room (fix: uniform uHand, hand bit in the outdoor thin half, no side records for hand cells) and the first shadow map pass = driver variant compile (27 ms; warm-up with the exact vertex state). After: max frame 17.9 ms; USER: 'viel besser beim Start'. Sweep afterwards found phantom trees indoors (catalogue not cleared indoors) -> objects outdoors only.

## Session 2026-11-11 late: room 54 'Nigel wirft Licht' = holes in hidden figures' shadows (Deck, Opus 5.5, no agents)
- User (rec_20261111_2006, throne room 54, frames 2760..3600): left of the soldier / Arthur it gets LIGHTER when Nigel walks past on their right.
- Method: `tools/lightshots.sh` bursts every 6 frames 2840..3320 with Lighting On / Off; a map of 'On brighter / darker than Off' and side-by-side crops. The figure shadows (blue in the map) lost a Nigel-shaped part whenever Nigel stood in front of a figure.
- Cause: `lk_lt_shadowed` / `lk_lt_figdir` (and GLSL shadowed / figBlock / figSun / the sun figure test) hit a figure's silhouette only where `lk_lt_spre` (tag of the TOP sprite pixel) equals the figure's tag; covered pixels belong to the front figure.
- Fix LK_LTHIDE v1 (`patch_lights.py`, `patch_gpulight.py`, `patch_gpucomp.py`): `lk_lt_spre2` = first hidden figure's tag per pixel (written where a sprite pixel is skipped because one is already there, cleared with lk_o_spr per frame); tests accept both tags. GPU class texture RGBA (class, class, hidden tag, tag); gpucomp's spe texture LA (tag, hidden tag). Switch `LANDSTALKER_LT_HIDE=0`.
- Checks: Lighting Off 81 / 81 frames bit exact; HIDE=0 vs the old build differs only in the HUD hearts / a few sparkles, which also differ between two runs of the same build (real-time noise); On: shadows whole (36 frames changed, all at overlapping figures).
- Mercator 632 stutter (same recording, frames 600..960), LK_GL5RING v1: windowed real-time replay script (copy of lightshots.sh without --headless, `LANDSTALKER_FRAMESTAT=1 LANDSTALKER_FRAMESTAT_SPIKE=12 LANDSTALKER_FRAMETIME=20`, second run = warm shader cache). Per frame `LANDSTALKER_GL5_TIME=4` (computes / visible without data) joined with the frametime lines showed every stutter frame = a frame with 4 ring blocks; `LANDSTALKER_GPULT_TIME=1` = timer query averages (light pass 0.2 ms, block passes 3..4 ms per frame); `/sys/class/drm/card0/device/pp_dpm_sclk` sampled every 50 ms: 200 MHz 88 % of the time. Switching models / room models / fine field / sword fine / bounce off changed nothing; Lighting Off removed the stutter. Fix: ring by GPU time (5 ms), ring 2 wide, nearest + ahead first. Stutter frames 620..999: 28 / 45 -> 10..15.
- GPU picture check for LK_LTHIDE: `LANDSTALKER_WINSHOT=2936,2996` windowed, HIDE=1 vs 0: the shadow left of Arthur / the soldier is there on the GPU path too.

ENTRYMS 12 / 18 / 22 tried: 18 = all blocks by 616, frames 27..37 ms.
